Derivative wrt h_p: terms:
- as first: if p = i+L with i>=0 => i=p-L, if 0≤p-L≤m-1 => h_p(1) derivative (1 - h_{p-L}) (provided p-L valid)

def _gold_min(h, i, j, lo, hi, base_count, iters=16, tol=1e-7):
    current = h.copy? no, modify in place.
    best_d=0.0; best_c=base_count
    phi = (sqrt(5)-1)/2? golden ratio 0.618
    # use interval [lo,hi]. Keep f at x1,x2.
    x1 = lo + (1-g)*(hi-lo)? typical.
    f1 = eval_at(h,i,j,x1)
    x2 = lo + g*(hi-lo)
    f2 = eval_at(h,i,j,x2)
    for ...:
       if f1 < f2: hi=x2; x2=x1; f2=f1; x1=lo+(1-g)*(hi-lo); f1=eval
       else: lo=x1; x1=x2; f1=f2; x2=lo+g*(hi-lo); f2=eval
    # after, candidate best of x1,x2 and perhaps midpoint/0; restore h before returning? We can leave at best if improving.
```

def _pair_golden(h, i, j, lo, hi, base_c, iters=18, tol_count=1e-7):
    ai, bj = float(h[i]), float(h[j])
    best_d=0.0; best_c=base_c
    xr = (np.sqrt(5.0)-1.0)/2.0
    x1 = lo + (1.0-xr)*(hi-lo)
    x2 = lo + xr*(hi-lo)
    h[i]=ai+x1; h[j]=bj-x1; f1=_cmax_count(h); update if f1<best
    h[i]=ai+x2; h[j]=bj-x2; f2=_cmax_count(h); update
    for...
       ... after computing new point, update best
    # maybe evaluate midpoint and lo/hi? Not needed convex; but include x1,x2 mid.
    Restore to best if best_d != 0.
    return best_c, best_d
```

Let's implement a hybrid:

A. Initialize h, c, count C.

B. Active-set LP descent (multi-coordinate):
- Compute c vector and active indices where c > C - slack (maybe top mA, e.g. first 64 or threshold). Compute gradient matrix G for active (or top mA). Solve LP to reduce max over active (not all) with small ρ. But LP is heavy; maybe not needed if pair L2.
- Instead use active pair search: For random pairs or sorted coordinates by sum of G, find pair with `np.all(G[:,i] - G[:,j] < -tol)` or `<tol>`. For such pair, exact pair line search (using full slope/quad) and update base vector. Repeat until no candidate in a scan.

This could be very fast and targeted.

def _slope_one(h, i):
    n=len(h); M=2*n-1
    sl=np.zeros(M)
    n1=n-1
    left=i
    right=n1-i
    if left:
        sl[n:n+left] += 1.0 - h[i-1::-1]
        sl[n-2:n-2-left:-1] -= h[i-1::-1]
    if right:
        sl[n:n+right] -= h[i+1:i+1+right]
        if i == 0:
            sl[n-2::-1] += 1.0 - h[1:n]
        else:
            sl[n-2:i-1:-1] += 1.0 - h[i+1:i+1+right]
    return sl
```

def _pair_descent(h, base, C_count, max_steps, sweep_idx, rng, time_budget):
  best = C_count
  improved = False

     if len(active)>128: active=active[:128]? Need ensure includes all maxima. Use sort desc and top min(128, count threshold C-0.01)
     G = _grad_active(h, active)

     # find candidate pairs
     candidates = 0; accepted=0
     # extreme pairs: sort low by col_max, high by col_min
     low_order = np.argsort(col_max)
     high_order = np.argsort(col_min)[::-1]

     checked = set? avoid too many.

```
def _line_search_positive(base, slope, quad, C0, dhi, iters=16):
   lo=0.0; hi=dhi; best_d=0; best_c=C0
   rho = (np.sqrt(5)-1)/2 #0.618
   x1 = lo + (1-rho)*(hi-lo)
   x2 = lo + rho*(hi-lo)
   f1 = _fmax(base,slope,quad,x1); update
   f2 = _fmax(...); update
   for ...:
      if f1 < f2: hi=x2; x2=x1; f2=f1; x1=lo+(1-rho)*(hi-lo); f1=eval
      else: lo=x1; x1=x2; f1=f2; x2=lo+rho*(hi-lo); f2=eval
   # also check mid maybe
   return best_c,best_d
```

Pseudo for pair phase function:

def _pair_phase(h, base, C, time_limit, rng, scales):
   for step in scales:
      if elapsed > limit: break
      acc_total=0; stall=0
      while time left and acc_total < max_acc_per_scale:
         act = _active_indices(base,C)
         G = _grad_active(h, act)
         col_max = G.max(0); col_min=G.min(0)
         found=False; accepted=0
         # candidates via extremes
         # For each high top 20:
         for j in np.argsort(col_min)[::-1][:20]:
             cand = np.where(col_max < col_min[j] - tol_g)[0]
             if cand.size:
                 i = cand[rng.randint(cand.size)]
                 if i==j: continue
                 dmax = min(step, 1-h[i], h[j])
                 if dmax <= 1e-10: continue
                 # line search positive
                 newC,d = _pair_line(h, base, i, j, 0, dmax, iters=14)
                 if newC < C - 1e-10:
                    apply
                    C = newC; acc_total+=1; accepted+=1
                    if accepted >= 20: break
                    found=True
          # random checks if not accepted?
          if accepted<20:
             for _ in range(1000):
                if time out break
                i,j = randint; if i< maybe
                diff=G[:,i]-G[:,j]
                if diff.max() < -tol_g:
                   dmax = min(step,1-h[i],h[j]); line search; if accept update...
                elif diff.min() > tol_g:
                   i,j = j,i; ...
             # after random, if no accepted this outer, stall++
         if no accepted in outer: break
      # recompute base after scale maybe
      base = _correlate_counts(h); C=base.max()
   return...
```

Slope computation allocation. We can combine pair slope and quad:
```
def _pair_slope_quad(h,i,j):
    sl = _slope_one(h,i) - _slope_one(h,j)
    q = np.zeros(2*n-1)
    if i != j:
       L=abs(i-j); q[n-1+L]=1.0; q[n-1-L]=1.0
       q[n-1] = -2.0 # c0 pair; always
    return sl,q
```

```
def _repair(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    target=0.5*len(h)
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # If sum not target, scale + adjust
    for _ in range(20):
       s=h.sum()
       if abs(s-target)<1e-13: break
       h *= target/s
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff>0: add to largest room
    else subtract from smallest h? Use argsort.
```

Now, performance of active G computation:
```
def _grad_active(h, idxs):
   n=len(h); m=len(idxs); G=np.empty((m,n))
   for r, idx in enumerate(idxs):
      row=np.zeros(n)
      s=idx-(n-1)
      if s>=0:
         L=s; mlen=n-L
         if mlen:
            row[L:n] += 1.0 - h[:mlen]
            row[:mlen] -= h[L:n]
      else:
         L=-s; mlen=n-L
         if mlen:
            row[:mlen] += 1.0 - h[L:n]
            row[L:n] -= h[:mlen]
      G[r]=row
   return G
```
For s=0, mlen=n: row[0:n]+=1-h; row[0:n]-=h => 1-2h? first row[n?] row[0:n] +=1-h; row[0:n]-=h works. Good.
Wait for s>=0 formula: row[L:n] += 1 - h[:mlen]. This sets gradient for first part at p=L..n-1: coeff 1-h[p-L] => h[0:mlen]. Good. row[:mlen] -= h[L:n]. Good.
For s<0: row[:mlen] +=1 - h[L:n]; row[L:n] -= h[:mlen]. Good.

```
def _lp_move(h, base, C, rho, n_slack, max_idx=200):
    n=len(h)
    # select constraints: top by base, at least exact active, up to max_idx, and base >= C - slack
    idx = np.where(base >= C - n_slack)[0]
    if len(idx) > max_idx: idx = idx[np.argsort(-base[idx])[:max_idx]]
    # ensure max included; if top selected after threshold maybe includes all active? use unique combine top max_idx and >=C-eps
    top = np.argsort(-base)[:max(1, min(max_idx, 2*n-1))]
    idx = np.unique(np.concatenate([top, np.where(base >= C - 1e-7)[0]]))[:max_idx] maybe could drop top? Simpler: idx = top[:max_idx] includes all top; but may miss near-active with slack. Use if len top < max_idx add threshold.
    G = _grad_active(h, idx)
    # Build A_ub: len(idx) x (n+1): G, -1. RHS C - base[idx]? Constraint G d - t <= C - base? Wait want linearized c_k = base_k + G_k d <= C + t. At d=0, t >= base_k - C (negative for lower, 0 for max). Minimize t. Row: G_k d - t <= C - base_k. RHS = C - base_k. Good. For max base=C RHS=0; lower positive.
    A_ub = np.empty((len(idx), n+1)); A_ub[:,:n]=G; A_ub[:,n]=-1.0
    b_ub = C - base[idx]
    A_eq=np.ones((1,n+1)); A_eq[-1]=0? Sum d=0 only d, t coeff0.
    bounds=[] for i in range(n): lo=max(-rho, -h[i]); hi=min(rho, 1-h[i]); bounds.append((lo,hi))
    bounds.append(( -C*0.05? -1, 1)) maybe [-1,1]
    res=linprog([0...1], A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds, method='highs')
    if res.success and res.x[-1] < -1e-7:
        d=res.x[:n]
        # line search alpha on [0,1]
        best=C; best_a=0
        for a in [1,0.8,0.6,0.5,0.4,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
            hn=h+a*d
            if valid; c=_cmax_count(hn); if c<best...
        if best<C-1e-7:
            h=h+best_a*d; base=_correlate_counts(h); return h,base,best
    return h,base,C
```

2 `_repair(h)`.
Implementation:
```
def _repair(h):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * len(h)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = h.sum()
    if s <= 1e-14:
        h.fill(0.5); return h
    for _ in range(30):
        s = h.sum()
        if abs(s-target) < 1e-13: break
        h *= target/s
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if diff > 1e-13:
        order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)
        for idx in order:
            add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
            if add <= 0: continue
            h[idx] += add; diff -= add
            if diff <= 1e-13: break
    elif diff < -1e-13:
        order = np.argsort(h)
        for idx in order:
            sub = min(-diff, h[idx])
            if sub <= 0: continue
            h[idx] -= sub; diff += sub
            if diff >= -1e-13: break
    # enforce exact if very small
    if abs(h.sum()-target) > 1e-12:
       h = np.clip(h,0,1)
       h += (target - h.sum())/len(h)
       h = np.clip(h,0,1)
       # distribute again? maybe okay
    return h
```
The final small adjust may break bounds; but if diff tiny, negligible. Better after distribution sum exact within 1e-12. If final adjust makes bounds slight, clip and sum error? Maybe skip final adjust. The distribution loop ensures exact if room exists. Since constraints feasible, should. Use tolerance 1e-12.
```

5 `_active_indices(base, C, cnt=80, tol=0.02)`:
```
def _active_indices(base, C, cap=80, tol=0.02):
    act = np.where(base >= C - tol)[0]
    if act.size == 0:
        act = np.array([int(np.argmax(base))])
    if act.size > cap:
        act = act[np.argsort(-base[act])[:cap]]
    return act
```

7 `_slope_one(h, i)` as derived, include center.
Let's write carefully:
```
def _slope_one(h, i):
    n = len(h); M = 2*n - 1
    sl = np.zeros(M)
    sl[n-1] = 1.0 - 2.0*h[i]
    left = i
    right = n - 1 - i
    if left > 0:
        # positive +L, L=1..left
        sl[n:n+left] += 1.0 - h[i-1::-1]
        # negative -L
        stop = n - 2 - left
        arr = h[i-1::-1]
        if stop < 0:
            sl[n-2::-1] -= arr
        else:
            sl[n-2:stop:-1] -= arr
    if right > 0:
        sl[n:n+right] -= h[i+1:i+1+right]
        arr = 1.0 - h[i+1:i+1+right]
        if i == 0:
            sl[n-2::-1] += arr
        else:
            sl[n-2:i-1:-1] += arr
    return sl
```
Check negative left slice length: if left >0 and stop = n-2-left. Example n=4,i=1,left=1 stop=1; slice `sl[2:1:-1]` index2 length1. Good. n=4,i=3,left=3 stop=-1; use `sl[2::-1]` indices2,1,0 length3 arr h[2::-1] length3. Good. n=2,i=1,left=1 stop=-1; sl[0::-1] index0 length1. Good.
Negative right slice: if i=0, sl[n-2::-1] length n-1=1 for n2: index0 arr 1-h[1]. Good. if i>0 stop=i-1: n=4,i=1 slice sl[2:0:-1] indices2,1 length2. Good. n=4,i=2,right=1 stop=1 slice sl[2:1:-1] index2 length1. good.
Positive slices: n=4,i=3,left3 slice sl[4:7] indices4,5,6; arr h[2::-1] length3. n=2,i=1 sl[2:3] index2.

8 `_pair_terms(h,i,j)`:
```
def _pair_terms(h,i,j):
    slope = _slope_one(h,i) - _slope_one(h,j)
    q = np.zeros(len(slope))
    q[h.shape[0]-1] = -2.0
    L = abs(i-j)
    if L > 0:
        n = len(h)
        q[n-1+L] = 1.0
        q[n-1-L] = 1.0
    return slope,q
```
Potential if L=0 not used; q center -2 would double count? If i=j, c0 with same variable moving? We don't call. Good.
For nonzero, if L=0 no.

10 `_line_search_positive(base, C0, slope, q, hi, iters)` top-level.
```
def _line_search_positive(base, C0, slope, q, hi, iters=14):
    if hi <= 1e-12: return C0, 0.0
    best_c = C0; best_d = 0.0
    # test fractions
    for fac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125,0.015625):
        d = hi*fac
        c = _fval(base,slope,q,d)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c=c; best_d=d
    # if hi not huge maybe golden over whole [0,hi]
    lo=0.0; hii=hi
    x1 = lo + (1.0 - _PHI)*(hii - lo)
    x2 = lo + _PHI*(hii - lo)
    if x1 <= 0: x1 = hi*1e-6
    if x2 >= hi: x2 = hi*(1-1e-6)
    f1 = _fval(...); update
    f2...
    for _ in range(iters):
        if f1 < f2:
            hii = x2; x2 = x1; f2 = f1
            x1 = lo + (1.0 - _PHI)*(hii - lo)
            f1 = _fval(...)
        else:
            lo = x1; x1 = x2; f1 = f2
            x2 = lo + _PHI*(hii - lo)
            f2 = _fval(...)
    # maybe check endpoints 0 and hi already
    return best_c,best_d
```
Since f may have minimum near 0, golden over [0,hi] works if unimodal with min interior or boundary? Standard golden for convex works, but if min at 0, it will shrink hi? If f1 < f2, hi=x2, eventually interval near 0; best may at x1 small not 0. We include 0. Good.
But if f is decreasing on entire interval (min at hi), golden will move lo forward and eventually hi. We evaluate hi in test. Good.
If f nonunimodal, not guaranteed but okay.
Could reduce iters if hi small: use 10. Use 14.

11 `_try_pair(h, base, C, i, j, step, iters=14)` returns (h,base,C, improved):
```
def _move_pair(h, base, C, i, j, dmax, iters=14):
    ai=float(h[i]); bj=float(h[j]); hi = min(dmax, 1.0-ai, bj)
    if hi <= 1e-13: return h,base,C,False
    slope,q = _pair_terms(h,i,j)
    nc, d = _line_search_positive(base, C, slope, q, hi, iters)
    if nc < C - 1e-10 and d > 1e-14:
        h[i] = ai + d; h[j] = bj - d
        base += slope*d + q*d*d
        C = float(base.max()) # or nc but use max for consistency
        return h, base, C, True
    return h,base,C,False
```
But if line search returns best_c from grid/golden but actual `base.max()` after update maybe slightly lower/higher due roundoff; use computed `C_new = float(np.max(base))` after update. The improvement test should use `nc < C - 1e-10`. Then update and set C = nc? Use max to be safe. If max differs >1e-8, maybe error. Use `C = float(base.max())`.

Could line search fail to find small improvement <1e-10 but there is; okay.

12 Pair phase. We must avoid nested loops too complex but okay.

```
def _pair_descent(h, base, C, t0, time_limit, rng, step, max_outer=50, iters=14):
    n=len(h); tol_g=1e-7; no_acc=0
    for outer in range(max_outer):
        if _elapsed(t0) >= time_limit: break
        act = _active_indices(base, C, cap=80, tol=0.02)
        G = _grad_active(h, act)
        if G.size == 0: break
        cmax = G.max(axis=0); cmin = G.min(axis=0)
        accepted = 0
        # try extreme candidates
        high = np.argsort(cmin)[::-1]
        for jj in high[:30]:
            j = int(jj)
            cand = np.where(cmax < cmin[j] - tol_g)[0]
            if cand.size == 0: continue
            # choose up to 4
            for _ in range(min(4, cand.size)):
                i = int(cand[rng.randint(cand.size)])
                if i == j: continue
                h,base,C,ok = _move_pair(h, base, C, i, j, step, iters)
                if ok:
                    accepted += 1
                    if accepted >= 12: break
            if accepted >= 12: break
        # random candidates if not too many
        if accepted < 12 and _elapsed(t0) < time_limit:
            for _ in range(2000):
                if _elapsed(t0) >= time_limit: break
                i = int(rng.randint(n)); j = int(rng.randint(n))
                if i == j: continue
                # use current h? G computed before accepts; if accepted, G stale. We can break to recompute after accepts.
                if accepted: break? Actually if accepted, we can stop random and recompute after loop. To keep candidate valid, after any accept, break outer? We can set accepted and break random, then outer while recompute.
                diff = G[:, i] - G[:, j]
                if diff.max() < -tol_g:
                    h,base,C,ok=... i,j
                elif diff.min() > tol_g:
                    h,base,C,ok=... j,i
                if ok:
                    accepted +=1
                    if accepted >= 12: break
        if accepted == 0:
            no_acc += 1
            if no_acc >= 3: break
        else:
            no_acc = 0
    return h,base,C
```
But if after extreme accept, we continue extreme loop using stale G; maybe okay for another candidate? To reduce wrong, accept limit 5 then break and recompute. Set if accepted >=5 break all. We'll use `if accepted >= 8: break` and set `outer_restart = True`.

Need nested break. We'll implement with small helper? No closures. Use flags.

for outer in range(max_outer):
   compute G

```

            if no_acc >= 5: break

def _lp_guided(h, base, C, t0, time_limit):
    n=len(h)
    rhos = [0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005]
    tries=0

       if _elapsed(t0) >= time_limit - 5: break
       for attempt in range(6):
          if time out break
          idx, G = _lp_constraints(h, base, C, cap=160)
          if idx.size < 1: break
          # build LP
          try: res=linprog(...)
          except: break
          if not res.success: continue
          d = res.x[:n]
          tval = res.x[-1]
          if tval > -1e-5: break
          # line search alpha
          best=C; best_a=0.0
          for a in (1.0,0.8,0.6,0.5,0.4,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01):
             hn = h + a*d
             if hn.min() < -1e-9 or hn.max() > 1+1e-9: continue

          tries+=1
          if C <= 0.3808*n/2? We don't know target count; could just continue.
       # if no improve with larger rho, smaller might still? Continue.
    return h,base,C

def _lp_constraints(h, base, C, cap=160, slack=0.25):

Need ensure no closures. Softplus functions top-level.

```
def _sobj(x, alpha, ml):
    n=len(x); dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,mode='full')*dx
    m=c.max()
    idx=np.argsort(c)[-ml:]
    s=alpha*(c[idx]-m); s-=s.max()
    return m + math.log(float(np.exp(s).sum()))/alpha
```

Let's craft `_pair_descent` code without closures. Need use `time_limit` absolute.
```
def _pair_descent(h, base, C, t0, time_limit, rng, step, raises=...):
    n = len(h)
    no_acc = 0
    max_outer = 100000  # until time/no acc
    tol_g = 1e-8
    while no_acc < 5 and _elapsed(t0) < time_limit:
        C = float(base.max())
        act = _active_indices(base, C, 80, 0.02)
        G = _grad_active(h, act)
        if G.size == 0: break
        cmax = G.max(axis=0)
        cmin = G.min(axis=0)
        accepted = False
        attempts = 0
        max_attempts = 80
        # extreme
        high = np.argsort(cmin)[::-1]
        for jj in high[:25]:
            if attempts >= max_attempts or _elapsed(t0) >= time_limit: break
            j = int(jj)
            cand = np.where(cmax < cmin[j] - tol_g)[0]
            if cand.size == 0: continue
            i = int(cand[rng.randint(cand.size)])
            if i == j: continue
            attempts += 1
            ok = _move_pair(h, base, C, i, j, step, 12)
            if ok:
                accepted = True; C = float(base.max()); break
        if not accepted and _elapsed(t0) < time_limit:
            for _ in range(400):
                if attempts >= max_attempts or _elapsed(t0) >= time_limit: break
                i = int(rng.randint(n)); j = int(rng.randint(n))
                if i == j: continue
                diff = G[:, i] - G[:, j]
                dm = float(diff.max()); dn = float(diff.min())
                if dm < -tol_g:
                    a,b=i,j
                elif dn > tol_g:
                    a,b=j,i
                else:
                    continue
                attempts += 1
                ok = _move_pair(h, base, C, a, b, step, 12)
                if ok:
                    accepted = True; C = float(base.max()); break
        if not accepted:
            no_acc += 1
        else:
            no_acc = 0
    return h, base, C
```
`_move_pair` currently returns only bool not updated C? It updates h,base but returns bool. In Python, multiple assignment from function that updates h,base in function cannot modify caller's unless h/base mutable and return. For h/base, if function modifies h in place and base in place (`base += ...` creates new local? `base +=` rebinds local, not caller! Important. In `_move_pair`, if we do `base += ...`, that rebinds local variable, does not update caller's `base` list/array. Need return updated base or update in place using `base[...] = base...`. To avoid copying, do `np.add(base, slope*d + q*d*d, out=base)` which modifies in place. Similarly h in place. Then caller sees changed base. h array is mutable; `h[i]=...` works. But `base +=` rebinds inside if called as `base = ...`; but if we use `np.add(base, update, out=base)`, caller's base array modified. Good.

So `_move_pair` should:
```
def _move_pair(h, base, C, i, j, dmax, iters=12):
    ...
    if improve:
       h[i] = ai + d; h[j] = bj - d
       np.add(base, slope*d + q*d*d, out=base)
       return True
    return False
```
It doesn't need return C. Caller recomputes `C = float(base.max())`.

Similarly `_fine_random` uses np.add out.

def _random_refine(h, base, C, t0, dl, rng, step):
  n=len(h)
  while _elapsed(t0) < dl:
    i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
    if i==j: continue
    slope,q=_pair_terms(h,i,j)
    ai=...; bj=...
    hp=min(step,1-ai,bj); hn=min(step,ai,1-bj)
    best=C; bd=0
    if hp>1e-12:
       for fac in (1,0.5,0.25):
          d=hp*fac; c=_fval(base,slope,q,d); update
    if hn>1e-12:
       for fac in (1,0.5,0.25):
          d=-hn*fac; c=_fval(...); update
    if best < C-1e-10 and bd !=0:
       h[i]=ai+bd; h[j]=bj-bd; np.add(base,slope*bd+q*bd*bd,out=base); C=best
```

`_repair`: Distribution loop with argsort O(n log n) each repair maybe multiple. Fine.
```
def _repair(h):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * len(h)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = h.sum()
    if s < 1e-14:
        h.fill(0.5); return h
    for _ in range(20):
        s = h.sum()
        if abs(s - target) < 1e-13: break
        h = (h * (target / s)).clip(0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if diff > 1e-13:
        order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)
        for idx in order:
            if diff <= 1e-13: break
            add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
            h[idx] += add; diff -= add
    elif diff < -1e-13:
        order = np.argsort(h)
        for idx in order:
            if diff >= -1e-13: break
            sub = min(-diff, h[idx])
            h[idx] -= sub; diff += sub
    return h
```
If scaling loop ends with sum off but no room? target feasible, should. Good.

So indices 558 down to 1 inclusive length558, not 0. Wait coordinate i=558, left neighbors L=1..558: p=i-L ranges 557 down to 0, last L=558 neighbor 0, index -558 = n-1-558 = 1. Yes index1, not 0. def _fval(base, slope, q, d):
    return float(np.max(base + slope * d + q * (d * d)))
```

def _line_search_positive(base, C0, slope, q, hi, iters=12):
    hi = float(hi)
    if hi <= 1e-12: return C0, 0.0
    best_c=C0; best_d=0.0
    for fac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625):
       d=hi*fac; c=_fval(...); if c<best_c: ...
    lo=0.0; hh=hi
    x1=lo+(1.0-_PHI)*(hh-lo)
    x2=lo+_PHI*(hh-lo)
    if x1 <=0: x1 = hh*0.1
    if x2 >= hh: x2=hh*0.9
    f1=_fval; update
    f2=...
    for _ in range(iters):
       if f1 < f2: ...
    return best_c,best_d
```
If best at 0, best_c remains C0. If f1/f2 slightly less due roundoff? okay.

`_move_pair`:
```
def _move_pair(h, base, C, i, j, dmax, iters=12):
    ai = float(h[i]); bj=float(h[j])
    hi = min(float(dmax), 1.0 - ai, bj)
    if hi <= 1e-13: return False
    slope, q = _pair_terms(h, i, j)
    nc, d = _line_search_positive(base, C, slope, q, hi, iters)
    if d <= 0.0: return False
    d = min(d, hi)
    if nc >= C - 1e-10: return False
    h[i] = ai + d
    h[j] = bj - d
    np.add(base, slope * d + q * (d*d), out=base)
    return True
```

def _pair_descent(...):
    n=len(h); no=0; tol_g=1e-7
    while no < 4 and _elapsed(t0) < time_limit:
        C = float(base.max())
        act = _active_indices(base,C,80,0.02)
        G = _grad_active(h, act)
        if G.size == 0: break
        cmax = G.max(axis=0); cmin = G.min(axis=0)
        ok = False; att=0
        high = np.argsort(cmin)[::-1]
        for jj in high[:20]:
           if att >= 60 or _elapsed(t0) >= time_limit: break
           j=int(jj)
           cand=np.where(cmax < cmin[j] - tol_g)[0]
           if cand.size ==0: continue
           i=int(cand[rng.randint(cand.size)])
           if i==j: continue
           att +=1
           if _move_pair(...): ok=True; break
        if not ok and _elapsed(t0) < time_limit:
           for _ in range(350):
              if att >= 60 or ...: break
              i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
              if i==j: continue
              diff=G[:,i]-G[:,j]
              # use max/min
              if diff.max() < -tol_g:
                 a,b=i,j
              elif diff.min() > tol_g:
                 a,b=j,i
              else: continue
              att +=1
              if _move_pair(... a,b): ok=True; break
        if not ok: no +=1
        else: no = 0
    return h, base, float(base.max())
```

`_lp_constraints`:
```
def _lp_constraints(h, base, C, cap=160, slack=0.35):
    m = base.size
    top = np.argsort(-base)[:min(cap, m)]
    extra = np.where(base >= C - slack)[0]
    idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, extra)))
    if idx.size > cap:
       idx = idx[np.argsort(-base[idx])[:cap]]
    return idx, _grad_active(h, idx)
```
If base max C, extra maybe many. top ensures. Use `min(cap, m)`.

def _lp_guided(h, base, C, t0, time_limit):
    n=len(h)
    rhos = (0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0005)
    for rho in rhos:
       if _elapsed(t0) >= time_limit - 2: break
       improved_rho = False
       for _ in range(5):
          if _elapsed(t0) >= time_limit - 2: break
          idx,G = _lp_constraints(h,base,C,160,0.35)
          m=idx.size
          if m == 0: break
          A = np.empty((m, n+1)); A[:, :n] = G; A[:, n] = -1.0
          b = C - base[idx]
          ceq = np.zeros(1); # no
          cobj = np.zeros(n+1); cobj[n]=1.0
          Aeq = np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1.0
          bounds=[]
          for i in range(n):
             lo = max(-rho, -h[i]); hi = min(rho, 1.0-h[i])
             bounds.append((lo,hi))
          bounds.append((-1.0,1.0))
          try:
             res = linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=[0.0], bounds=bounds, disp=False)
          except Exception:
             break
          if res is None or not res.success: continue
          d = res.x[:n]
          if res.x[-1] > -1e-6:
             break
          best=C; ba=0.0
          for a in (1.0,0.8,0.6,0.5,0.4,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01):
             if _elapsed(t0) >= time_limit: break
             hn = h + a*d
             if hn.min() < -1e-8 or hn.max() > 1.0+1e-8: continue
             cn = float(np.max(np.correlate(hn, 1.0-hn, mode='full')))
             if cn < best:
                best=cn; ba=a
          if ba > 0.0 and best < C - 1e-7:
             h = _repair(h + ba*d)
             base = _base_counts(h)
             C = float(base.max())
             improved_rho = True
          else:
             break
       # after rho, maybe if C improved continue, else smaller? continue even if not? yes smaller maybe works.
    return h, base, C
```

def _random_refine(h, base, C, t0, time_limit, rng, step):
    n=len(h)
    while _elapsed(t0) < time_limit:
        i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
        if i==j: continue
        slope,q = _pair_terms(h,i,j)
        ai=float(h[i]); bj=float(h[j])

        if hn > 1e-12:

           np.add(base, slope*bd + q*(bd*bd), out=base)
           C=best

    return h, base, float(base.max())

def _line_search_positive(...):
    hi = float(hi)
    if hi <= 1e-12: return C0,0
    best_c=C0; best_d=0.0
    ds = (0.0, hi, 0.5*hi, 0.25*hi, 0.125*hi, 0.0625*hi)
    for d in ds:
        c = _fval(base,slope,q,d)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c=c; best_d=d
    if best_d <= 0.0:
        lo=0.0; hh=0.25*hi
    elif best_d >= 0.75*hi:
        lo=0.75*hi; hh=hi
    else:
        lo=best_d - 0.25*hi
        if lo < 0: lo=0
        hh=best_d + 0.25*hi
        if hh > hi: hh=hi
    if hh - lo <= 1e-13: return best_c,best_d
    x1=lo+(1-_PHI)*(hh-lo); x2=lo+_PHI*(hh-lo)
    if x1 <= lo: x1=(lo+hh)*0.5
    if x2 >= hh: x2=(lo+hh)*0.5
    f1=_fval; f2=_fval; update
    for _ in range(10): ...
    return best_c,best_d

Let's write `_grad_active` efficiently:
```
def _grad_active(h, idxs):
    n = len(h)
    m = idxs.size
    G = np.empty((m, n), dtype=float)
    for r in range(m):
        idx = int(idxs[r])
        row = np.zeros(n)
        s = idx - (n - 1)
        if s >= 0:
            L = s
            mlen = n - L
            if mlen > 0:
                row[L:n] += 1.0 - h[:mlen]
                row[:mlen] -= h[L:n]
        else:
            L = -s
            mlen = n - L
            if mlen > 0:
                row[:mlen] += 1.0 - h[L:n]
                row[L:n] -= h[:mlen]
        G[r] = row
    return G
```
If m=0, `np.empty((0,n))` and loop none. Good. But `idxs` may be list? We pass np array. In `_lp_constraints`, `idx` numpy.

top = np.argsort(-base)[:cap]
extra = np.where(base >= C - slack)[0]

```

Let's draft final code in analysis to ensure syntax:

```
import time, math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import linprog

_PHI = (math.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0

def _elapsed(t0):
    return time.time() - t0

def _repair(h):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * len(h)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    if h.sum() < 1e-14:
        h.fill(0.5)
        return h
    for _ in range(20):
        s = h.sum()
        if abs(s - target) < 1e-13:
            break
        h = np.clip(h * (target / s), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if diff > 1e-13:
        for idx in np.argsort(1.0 - h):
            if diff <= 1e-13:
                break
            a = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
            h[idx] += a
            diff -= a
    elif diff < -1e-13:
        for idx in np.argsort(h):
            if diff >= -1e-13:
                break
            a = min(-diff, h[idx])
            h[idx] -= a
            diff += a
    return h

def _base_counts(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')

def _active_indices(base, C, cap=80, tol=0.02):
    act = np.where(base >= C - tol)[0]
    if act.size == 0:
        act = np.array([int(np.argmax(base)))])
    if act.size > cap:
        act = act[np.argsort(-base[act])[:cap]]
    return act

def _grad_active(h, idxs):
    n = len(h)
    m = idxs.size
    G = np.empty((m, n), dtype=float)
    for r in range(m):
        idx = int(idxs[r])
        row = np.zeros(n)
        s = idx - (n - 1)
        if s >= 0:
            L = s
            ml = n - L
            if ml > 0:
                row[L:n] += 1.0 - h[:ml]
                row[:ml] -= h[L:n]
        else:
            L = -s
            ml = n - L
            if ml > 0:
                row[:ml] += 1.0 - h[L:n]
                row[L:n] -= h[:ml]
        G[r] = row
    return G

def _slope_one(h, i):
    n = len(h)
    sl = np.zeros(2 * n - 1)
    sl[n - 1] = 1.0 - 2.0 * h[i]
    left = i
    right = n - 1 - i
    if left > 0:
        arr = h[i - 1::-1]
        sl[n:n + left] += 1.0 - arr
        stop = n - 2 - left
        if stop < 0:
            sl[n - 2::-1] -= arr
        else:
            sl[n - 2:stop:-1] -= arr
    if right > 0:
        arr = h[i + 1:i + 1 + right]
        sl[n:n + right] -= arr
        arr2 = 1.0 - arr
        if i == 0:
            sl[n - 2::-1] += arr2
        else:
            sl[n - 2:i - 1:-1] += arr2
    return sl

def _pair_terms(h, i, j):
    slope = _slope_one(h, i) - _slope_one(h, j)
    q = np.zeros(len(slope))
    n = len(h)
    q[n - 1] = -2.0
    L = abs(i - j)
    if L > 0:
        q[n - 1 + L] = 1.0
        q[n - 1 - L] = 1.0
    return slope, q

def _fval(base, slope, q, d):
    return float(np.max(base + slope * d + q * (d * d)))

def _line_search_positive(base, C0, slope, q, hi, iters):
    hi = float(hi)
    if hi <= 1e-12:
        return C0, 0.0
    best_c = C0
    best_d = 0.0
    for d in (0.0, hi, 0.5 * hi, 0.25 * hi, 0.125 * hi, 0.0625 * hi):
        c = _fval(base, slope, q, d)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_d = d
    if best_d <= 0.0:
        lo = 0.0
        hhi = 0.25 * hi
    elif best_d >= 0.75 * hi:
        lo = 0.75 * hi
        hhi = hi
    else:
        lo = max(0.0, best_d - 0.25 * hi)
        hhi = min(hi, best_d + 0.25 * hi)
    if hhi - lo > 1e-12:
        x1 = lo + (1.0 - _PHI) * (hhi - lo)
        x2 = lo + _PHI * (hhi - lo)
        if x1 <= lo:
            x1 = 0.5 * (lo + hhi)
        if x2 >= hhi:
            x2 = 0.5 * (lo + hhi)
        f1 = _fval(base, slope, q, x1)
        f2 = _fval(base, slope, q, x2)
        if f1 < best_c:
            best_c = f1
            best_d = x1
        if f2 < best_c:
            best_c = f2
            best_d = x2
        for _ in range(iters):
            if f1 < f2:
                hhi = x2
                x2 = x1
                f2 = f1
                x1 = lo + (1.0 - _PHI) * (hhi - lo)
                f1 = _fval(base, slope, q, x1)
            else:
                lo = x1
                x1 = x2
                f1 = f2
                x2 = lo + _PHI * (hhi - lo)
                f2 = _fval(base, slope, q, x2)
            if f1 < best_c:
                best_c = f1
                best_d = x1
            if f2 < best_c:
                best_c = f2
                best_d = x2
    return best_c, best_d

def _move_pair(h, base, C, i, j, dmax, iters=12):
    ai = float(h[i])
    bj = float(h[j])
    hi = min(float(dmax), 1.0 - ai, bj)
    if hi <= 1e-13:
        return False
    slope, q = _pair_terms(h, i, j)
    nc, d = _line_search_positive(base, C, slope, q, hi, iters)
    if d <= 0.0 or nc >= C - 1e-9:
        return False
    d = min(d, hi)
    h[i] = ai + d
    h[j] = bj - d
    np.add(base, slope * d + q * (d * d), out=base)
    return True

def _pair_descent(h, base, C, t0, time_limit, rng, step):
    n = len(h)
    no = 0
    tol_g = 1e-7
    while no < 4 and _elapsed(t0) < time_limit:
        C = float(base.max())
        act = _active_indices(base, C, 80, 0.02)
        G = _grad_active(h, act)
        if G.size == 0:
            break
        cmax = G.max(axis=0)
        cmin = G.min(axis=0)
        ok = False
        att = 0
        high = np.argsort(cmin)[::-1]
        for jj in high[:20]:
            if att >= 60 or _elapsed(t0) >= time_limit:
                break
            j = int(jj)
            cand = np.where(cmax < cmin[j] - tol_g)[0]
            if cand.size == 0:
                continue
            i = int(cand[rng.randint(cand.size)])
            if i == j:
                continue
            att += 1
            if _move_pair(h, base, C, i, j, step, 12):
                ok = True
                break
        if not ok and _elapsed(t0) < time_limit:
            for _ in range(300):
                if att >= 60 or _elapsed(t0) >= time_limit:
                    break
                i = int(rng.randint(n))
                j = int(rng.randint(n))
                if i == j:
                    continue
                diff = G[:, i] - G[:, j]
                dm = float(diff.max())
                dn = float(diff.min())
                if dm < -tol_g:
                    a, b = i, j
                elif dn > tol_g:
                    a, b = j, i
                else:
                    continue
                att += 1
                if _move_pair(h, base, C, a, b, step, 12):
                    ok = True
                    break
        if ok:
            no = 0
        else:
            no += 1
    return h, base, float(base.max())
```
Line count ~150. Good.

LP compact:
```
def _lp_constraints(h, base, C, cap=120, slack=0.3):
    top = np.argsort(-base)[:cap]
    extra = np.where(base >= C - slack)[0]
    idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, extra)))
    if idx.size > cap:
        idx = idx[np.argsort(-base[idx])[:cap]]
    return idx, _grad_active(h, idx)

def _lp_guided(h, base, C, t0, time_limit):
    n = len(h)
    for rho in (0.03, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001):
        for _ in range(4):
            if _elapsed(t0) >= time_limit:
                break
            idx, G = _lp_constraints(h, base, C, 120, 0.30)
            m = idx.size
            if m == 0:
                break
            A = np.empty((m, n + 1))
            A[:, :n] = G
            A[:, n] = -1.0
            b = C - base[idx]
            cutoff = np.zeros(n + 1)
            cutoff[n] = 1.0
            Aeq = np.zeros((1, n + 1))
            Aeq[0, :n] = 1.0
            bounds = []
            for i in range(n):
                bounds.append((max(-rho, -h[i]), min(rho, 1.0 - h[i])))
            bounds.append((-1.0, 1.0))
            try:
                res = linprog(cutoff, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=[0.0], bounds=bounds, disp=False)
            except Exception:
                break
            if res is None or not res.success or res.x[-1] > -1e-6:
                break
            d = res.x[:n]
            best = C
            ba = 0.0
            for a in (1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02):
                if _elapsed(t0) >= time_limit:
                    break
                hn = h + a * d
                if hn.min() < -1e-8 or hn.max() > 1.0 + 1e-8:
                    continue
                cn = float(np.max(_base_counts(hn)))
                if cn < best:
                    best = cn
                    ba = a
            if ba > 0.0 and best < C - 1e-8:
                h = _repair(h + ba * d)
                base = _base_counts(h)
                C = float(base.max())
            else:
                break
    return h, base, C
```
Line count +50 = 200.

Random refine:
```
def _random_refine(h, base, C, t0, time_limit, rng, step):
    n = len(h)
    while _elapsed(t0) < time_limit:
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n))
        if i == j:
            continue
        slope, q = _pair_terms(h, i, j)
        ai = float(h[i]); bj = float(h[j])
        hp = min(float(step), 1.0 - ai, bj)
        hn = min(float(step), ai, 1.0 - bj)
        best = C
        bd = 0.0
        if hp > 1e-12:
            for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
                d = hp * fac
                c = _fval(base, slope, q, d)
                if c < best:
                    best = c; bd = d
        if hn > 1e-12:
            for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
                d = -hn * fac
                c = _fval(base, slope, q, d)
                if c < best:
                    best = c; bd = d
        if bd != 0.0 and best < C - 1e-9:
            h[i] = ai + bd
            h[j] = bj - bd
            np.add(base, slope * bd + q * (bd * bd), out=base)
            C = float(base.max())
    return h, base, C
```
+25 =225.

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = _repair(np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy())
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    base = _base_counts(h)
    C = float(base.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_C = C
    hard = t0 + budget_s
    pair_end = t0 + min(max(budget_s - 300, 30), 700)
    if pair_end > hard - 200:
        pair_end = hard - 200
    scales = ((0.05, 80), (0.02, 100), (0.01, 100), (0.005, 90), (0.002, 80), (0.001, 70), (0.0005, 70), (0.0002, 60), (0.0001, 50), (5.0e-5, 40), (2.0e-5, 30))
    for step, add in scales:
        if _elapsed(t0) >= pair_end:
            break
        end = min(pair_end, time.time() + add)
        h, base, C = _pair_descent(h, base, C, t0, end, rng, step)

        base = _base_counts(h)
        C = float(base.max())

            best_C = C
            best_h = h.copy()
        else:
            h = best_h.copy()
            base = _base_counts(h)
            C = best_C

                best_C = C
                best_h = h.copy()

                h = best_h.copy()
                base = _base_counts(h)
                C = best_C
    h = _repair(best_h)
    base = _base_counts(h)
    c5 = float(base.max()) * dx
    return h, c5, n

```python
import time, math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import linprog

_PHI = (math.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0

def _elapsed(t0):
    return time.time() - t0

def _repair(h):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * len(h)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    if h.sum() < 1e-14:
        h.fill(0.5)
        return h
    for _ in range(20):
        s = h.sum()
        if abs(s - target) < 1e-12:
            break
        h = np.clip(h * (target / s), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if diff > 1e-12:
        for idx in np.argsort(1.0 - h):
            if diff <= 1e-12:
                break
            a = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
            h[idx] += a
            diff -= a
    elif diff < -1e-12:
        for idx in np.argsort(h):
            if diff >= -1e-12:
                break
            a = min(-diff, h[idx])
            h[idx] -= a
            diff += a
    return h

def _base_counts(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')

def _active_indices(base, C, cap=80, tol=0.02):
    act = np.where(base >= C - tol)[0]
    if act.size == 0:
        act = np.array([int(np.argmax(base))])
    if act.size > cap:
        act = act[np.argsort(-base[act])[:cap]]
    return act

def _grad_active(h, idxs):
    n = len(h)
    m = idxs.size
    G = np.empty((m, n), dtype=float)
    for r in range(m):
        idx = int(idxs[r])
        row = np.zeros(n)
        s = idx - (n - 1)
        if s >= 0:
            L = s
            ml = n - L
            if ml > 0:
                row[L:n] += 1.0 - h[:ml]
                row[:ml] -= h[L:n]
        else:
            L = -s
            ml = n - L
            if ml > 0:
                row[:ml] += 1.0 - h[L:n]
                row[L:n] -= h[:ml]
        G[r] = row
    return G

def _slope_one(h, i):
    n = len(h)
    sl = np.zeros(2 * n - 1)
    sl[n - 1] = 1.0 - 2.0 * h[i]
    left = i
    right = n - 1 - i
    if left > 0:
        arr = h[i - 1::-1]
        sl[n:n + left] += 1.0 - arr
        stop = n - 2 - left
        if stop < 0:
            sl[n - 2::-1] -= arr
        else:
            sl[n - 2:stop:-1] -= arr
    if right > 0:
        arr = h[i + 1:i + 1 + right]
        sl[n:n + right] -= arr
        arr2 = 1.0 - arr
        if i == 0:
            sl[n - 2::-1] += arr2
        else:
            sl[n - 2:i - 1:-1] += arr2
    return sl

def _pair_terms(h, i, j):
    slope = _slope_one(h, i) - _slope_one(h, j)
    q = np.zeros(len(slope))
    n = len(h)
    q[n - 1] = -2.0
    L = abs(i - j)
    if L > 0:
        q[n - 1 + L] = 1.0
        q[n - 1 - L] = 1.0
    return slope, q

def _fval(base, slope, q, d):
    return float(np.max(base + slope * d + q * (d * d)))

def _line_search_positive(base, C0, slope, q, hi):
    hi = float(hi)
    if hi <= 1e-12:
        return C0, 0.0
    best_c = C0
    best_d = 0.0
    for d in (0.0, hi, 0.5 * hi, 0.25 * hi, 0.125 * hi, 0.0625 * hi):
        c = _fval(base, slope, q, d)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_d = d
    if best_d <= 0.0:
        lo = 0.0
        hhi = 0.25 * hi
    elif best_d >= 0.75 * hi:
        lo = 0.75 * hi
        hhi = hi
    else:
        lo = max(0.0, best_d - 0.25 * hi)
        hhi = min(hi, best_d + 0.25 * hi)
    if hhi - lo > 1e-12:
        x1 = lo + (1.0 - _PHI) * (hhi - lo)
        x2 = lo + _PHI * (hhi - lo)
        if x1 <= lo:
            x1 = 0.5 * (lo + hhi)
        if x2 >= hhi:
            x2 = 0.5 * (lo + hhi)
        f1 = _fval(base, slope, q, x1)
        f2 = _fval(base, slope, q, x2)
        if f1 < best_c:
            best_c = f1
            best_d = x1
        if f2 < best_c:
            best_c = f2
            best_d = x2
        for _ in range(10):
            if f1 < f2:
                hhi = x2
                x2 = x1
                f2 = f1
                x1 = lo + (1.0 - _PHI) * (hhi - lo)
                f1 = _fval(base, slope, q, x1)
            else:
                lo = x1
                x1 = x2
                f1 = f2
                x2 = lo + _PHI * (hhi - lo)
                f2 = _fval(base, slope, q, x2)
            if f1 < best_c:
                best_c = f1
                best_d = x1
            if f2 < best_c:
                best_c = f2
                best_d = x2
    return best_c, best_d

def _move_pair(h, base, C, i, j, dmax):
    ai = float(h[i])
    bj = float(h[j])
    hi = min(float(dmax), 1.0 - ai, bj)
    if hi <= 1e-13:
        return False
    slope, q = _pair_terms(h, i, j)
    nc, d = _line_search_positive(base, C, slope, q, hi)
    if d <= 0.0 or nc >= C - 1e-9:
        return False
    d = min(d, hi)
    h[i] = ai + d
    h[j] = bj - d
    np.add(base, slope * d + q * (d * d), out=base)
    return True

def _pair_descent(h, base, C, t0, time_limit, rng, step):
    n = len(h)
    no = 0
    tol_g = 1e-7
    while no < 4 and _elapsed(t0) < time_limit:
        C = float(base.max())
        act = _active_indices(base, C, 80, 0.02)
        G = _grad_active(h, act)
        if G.size == 0:
            break
        cmax = G.max(axis=0)
        cmin = G.min(axis=0)
        ok = False
        att = 0
        high = np.argsort(cmin)[::-1]
        for jj in high[:20]:
            if att >= 60 or _elapsed(t0) >= time_limit:
                break
            j = int(jj)
            cand = np.where(cmax < cmin[j] - tol_g)[0]
            if cand.size == 0:
                continue
            i = int(cand[rng.randint(cand.size)])
            if i == j:
                continue
            att += 1
            if _move_pair(h, base, C, i, j, step):
                ok = True
                break
        if not ok and _elapsed(t0) < time_limit:
            for _ in range(300):
                if att >= 60 or _elapsed(t0) >= time_limit:
                    break
                i = int(rng.randint(n))
                j = int(rng.randint(n))
                if i == j:
                    continue
                diff = G[:, i] - G[:, j]
                dm = float(diff.max())
                dn = float(diff.min())
                if dm < -tol_g:
                    a, b = i, j
                elif dn > tol_g:
                    a, b = j, i
                else:
                    continue
                att += 1
                if _move_pair(h, base, C, a, b, step):
                    ok = True
                    break
        if ok:
            no = 0
        else:
            no += 1
    return h, base, float(base.max())

def _lp_constraints(h, base, C, cap=120, slack=0.30):
    top = np.argsort(-base)[:cap]
    extra = np.where(base >= C - slack)[0]
    idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, extra)))
    if idx.size > cap:
        idx = idx[np.argsort(-base[idx])[:cap]]
    return idx, _grad_active(h, idx)

def _lp_guided(h, base, C, t0, time_limit):
    n = len(h)
    for rho in (0.03, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001):
        for _ in range(4):
            if _elapsed(t0) >= time_limit:
                break
            idx, G = _lp_constraints(h, base, C, 120, 0.30)
            m = idx.size
            if m == 0:
                break
            A = np.empty((m, n + 1))
            A[:, :n] = G
            A[:, n] = -1.0
            b = C - base[idx]
            cutoff = np.zeros(n + 1)
            cutoff[n] = 1.0
            Aeq = np.zeros((1, n + 1))
            Aeq[0, :n] = 1.0
            bounds = []
            for i in range(n):
                bounds.append((max(-rho, -h[i]), min(rho, 1.0 - h[i])))
            bounds.append((-1.0, 1.0))
            try:
                res = linprog(cutoff, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=[0.0], bounds=bounds, disp=False)
            except Exception:
                break
            if res is None or not res.success or res.x[-1] > -1e-6:
                break
            d = res.x[:n]
            best = C
            ba = 0.0
            for a in (1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02):
                if _elapsed(t0) >= time_limit:
                    break
                hn = h + a * d
                if hn.min() < -1e-8 or hn.max() > 1.0 + 1e-8:
                    continue
                cn = float(np.max(_base_counts(hn)))
                if cn < best:
                    best = cn
                    ba = a
            if ba > 0.0 and best < C - 1e-8:
                h = _repair(h + ba * d)
                base = _base_counts(h)
                C = float(base.max())
            else:
                break
    return h, base, C

def _random_refine(h, base, C, t0, time_limit, rng, step):
    n = len(h)
    while _elapsed(t0) < time_limit:
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n))
        if i == j:
            continue
        slope, q = _pair_terms(h, i, j)
        ai = float(h[i])
        bj = float(h[j])
        hp = min(float(step), 1.0 - ai, bj)
        hn = min(float(step), ai, 1.0 - bj)
        best = C
        bd = 0.0
        if hp > 1e-12:
            for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
                d = hp * fac
                c = _fval(base, slope, q, d)
                if c < best:
                    best = c
                    bd = d
        if hn > 1e-12:
            for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
                d = -hn * fac
                c = _fval(base, slope, q, d)
                if c < best:
                    best = c
                    bd = d
        if bd != 0.0 and best < C - 1e-9:
            h[i] = ai + bd
            h[j] = bj - bd
            np.add(base, slope * bd + q * (bd * bd), out=base)
            C = float(base.max())
    return h, base, C

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        init = initial_h_values
    except NameError:
        z = np.arange(560)
        init = (z % 2 == 0).astype(float)
    h = _repair(np.array(init, dtype=float).copy())
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    base = _base_counts(h)
    C = float(base.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_C = C
    hard = t0 + budget_s

    pair_end = t0 + min(max(budget_s - 250, 30), 780)
    if pair_end > hard - 180:
        pair_end = hard - 180
    scales = ((0.05, 80), (0.02, 100), (0.01, 100), (0.005, 90), (0.002, 80),
              (0.001, 70), (0.0005, 70), (0.0002, 60), (0.0001, 50),
              (5.0e-5, 40), (2.0e-5, 30))
    for step, add in scales:
        if _elapsed(t0) >= pair_end:
            break
        end = min(pair_end, time.time() + add)
        h, base, C = _pair_descent(h, base, C, t0, end, rng, step)
        h = _repair(h)
        base = _base_counts(h)
        C = float(base.max())
        if C < best_C:
            best_C = C
            best_h = h.copy()
        else:
            h = best_h.copy()
            base = _base_counts(h)
            C = best_C

    if _elapsed(t0) < hard - 220:
        end = min(hard - 120, time.time() + 220)
        h, base, C = _lp_guided(h, base, C, t0, end)
        h = _repair(h)
        base = _base_counts(h)
        C = float(base.max())
        if C < best_C:
            best_C = C
            best_h = h.copy()
        else:
            h = best_h.copy()
            base = _base_counts(h)
            C = best_C

    if _elapsed(t0) < hard - 100 and best_C > (0.3808 / dx):
        for amp in (0.015, 0.008):
            if _elapsed(t0) >= hard - 50:
                break
            end = min(hard - 40, time.time() + 60)
            ht = best_h + amp * rng.randn(n)
            ht -= ht.mean()
            ht = _repair(ht)
            bt = _base_counts(ht)
            Ct = float(bt.max())
            if Ct < best_C + (0.005 / dx):
                ht, bt, Ct = _pair_descent(ht, bt, Ct, t0, end, rng, 0.002)
                ht = _repair(ht)
                bt = _base_counts(ht)
                Ct = float(bt.max())
                if Ct < best_C:
                    best_C = Ct
                    best_h = ht.copy()

    if _elapsed(t0) < hard - 80:
        for step, add in ((2.0e-4, 40), (1.0e-4, 35), (5.0e-5, 30), (2.0e-5, 25)):
            if _elapsed(t0) >= hard - 30:
                break
            end = min(hard - 30, time.time() + add)
            h, base, C = _random_refine(h, base, C, t0, end, rng, step)
            h = _repair(h)
            base = _base_counts(h)
            C = float(base.max())
            if C < best_C:
                best_C = C
                best_h = h.copy()
            else:
                h = best_h.copy()
                base = _base_counts(h)
                C = best_C

    h = _repair(best_h)
    c5 = float(np.max(_base_counts(h))) * dx
    return h, float(c5), n
```
